### Existing collective contexts

- Le☆S☆Ca / `UNIT-0014`: The established three members carry different private models into shared work, suffer a bounded coordination cost and make a jointly voiced wish to value and remain the unit. - Ume / `IDENTITY-0025` / `CHAR-0022` / kujo-ume: Her desire for an unequivocal rank drives the wager, but her participation is not one unchanging pose of aggression. She makes shaved ice skillfully, permits Hina's withdrawal, learns unfamiliar rules, reveals detailed peer knowledge, fails at a sea search, explicitly recognizes Haru as friend and her bandmates as family-like, then contests the ruling and renews revenge. ### Source-named transient collective

### Existing collective context

- Nanasta / `UNIT-0002`: The family concerns Christmas work, an attempted change in group norms and a local agreement to continue preparations. The enlarged hobby community is first declared successful and then contested through its burdens. No all-member conversion, permanent governance rule, completed party or new formal unit is established. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0260.json__891d92c899f977cb?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0260.json__891d92c899f977cb?doc=primary&log=81#/Pages/81` (P0–81); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0261.json__bdf30f40ee64cfad?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0261.json__bdf30f40ee64cfad?doc=primary&log=89#/Pages/89` (P0–89); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0262.json__181fac768963af1f?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0262.json__181fac768963af1f?doc=primary&log=65#/Pages/65` (P0–65); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0263.json__f3df4cf84b882e9f?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0263.json__f3df4cf84b882e9f?doc=primary&log=74#/Pages/74` (P0–74); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0264.json__95b47137af450c7c?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0264.json__95b47137af450c7c?doc=primary&log=89#/Pages/89` (P0–89); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0265.json__9284fd8ec4bd01f0?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0265.json__9284fd8ec4bd01f0?doc=primary&log=102#/Pages/102` (P0–102).
